## BSAT baseline: bsat_restart_20261004_102638

Status: RUNNING at last observation. PID 2467579 is node-local and may later disappear or be reused; verify its command before acting.

Purpose: restart the existing physical RadarGS pipeline on the new BSAT data, with a fixed held-out CPI split. This is a baseline, not a claim that the old best method has been reproduced.

### Data audit and split

- Snapshot found 2,378 CPI directories, each with one 751 x 751 PNG, view_angle.npy, and _COMPLETE.json.
- Select complete directories with geometry and exactly one PNG, sorted lexicographically (zero-padded CPI names).
- Zero-based sorted index i % 5 == 4 is held out; all others train: 1,903 train, 475 held out.
- Exact lists are authoritative; do not regenerate them if new data arrives.
- Each CPI is an ISAR frame, NOT an independent physical encounter. Do not label this run K=2378 encounters.
- The background design described 2,428 CPIs; this snapshot contains 2,378. Missing coverage/completeness should be audited before claiming the full planned sequence was used.
- view_angle.npy contains one [range-axis CAD xyz, cross-range-axis CAD xyz] row; existing radar_gs/data.py reads it directly.
- Simulator source checked: 300 GHz carrier, 10 GHz bandwidth, PRF 1400, 751 pulses, CPI aperture 1.929 degrees. Existing native pixel scales were retained; no new PSF model or phase recovery was introduced.
- Interleaved held-out CPIs test nearby-view interpolation, not entirely unseen days/encounters. A blocked temporal/aspect split would be a separate stronger test.

### Training arrangement

- Entry: run_reconstruction.py; base config: configs/full_backprojection.json.
- Overrides: frames_per_encounter=1, images_per_encounter=1, los_angle_deg=0, iters=12000, views_per_batch=4, checkpoint_interval=1000, preview_interval=1000, lambda_surface_gap=0, seed=42, device=cuda.
- Four-stage boundaries in resolved config: stage_a_fraction=0.15, stage_b_fraction=0.5, stage_c_fraction=0.8 (approximately 1800/6000/9600 iterations).
- Initial Gaussian count: 10,000. OMP_NUM_THREADS=8 and MKL_NUM_THREADS=8.
- Original code/config files were not edited. The child process was launched detached with stdout/stderr redirected to train.log.
- Exact argv and split counts: launch.json. Effective complete parameters: training/run_config.json. Read these instead of reconstructing settings from this summary.

### Last verified observation

- Loaded 1,903 selected PNG views; iteration 1100, Stage A, G=10000, loss=0.031557, logged sim=0.93249.
- An earlier GPU snapshot at iteration 500: utilization 71%, 1343 MiB, 42 C.
- No training Traceback/OOM observed. These are historical snapshots, not live status.
- Logged sim/loss are training diagnostics, NOT held-out PSNR/SSIM. No final held-out or aligned-GT evaluation has been run/submitted in this restart.

### Next steps and safe handoff

1. Check train.log and process activity; leave a healthy run alone. Do not duplicate or restart it merely because loading/backprojection initially uses CPU.
2. Verify periodic checkpoints/previews and eventual completion at 12,000; inspect errors and allocation expiry if it stops early.
3. After training, evaluate exclusively on heldout_cpis.txt with the same preprocessing and calibration. Inspect evaluator CLI: it may need a generated preset or explicit evaluation-view JSON rather than this text list.
4. For Chamfer/HD95/etc., first locate and verify BSAT GT CAD mesh, units and coordinate alignment. No aligned GT path has yet been verified; do not reuse an unrelated Skynet mesh.
5. Compare matched prediction/GT amplitudes and support geometry; inspect fish-scale patterns, central diffusion and edge leakage. Do not infer unseen-view improvement from training sim.
6. Append dated observations, evaluation commands, metric file paths and conclusions here. Preserve this record and historical outputs; do not silently rewrite the split or parameters.
